|U|=u\end{subarray}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\subseteq R\\
|U|=u\end{subarray}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\subseteq R\\
|U|=u\end{subarray}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\right]]}]}~\leq~\eta,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}U\subseteq R,|U|=u\\
\sigma\sim\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\end{subarray}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\subseteq R,|U|=u\\
\sigma\sim\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\end{subarray}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\subseteq R,|U|=u\\
\sigma\sim\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\end{subarray}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\subseteq R,|U|=u\\
\sigma\sim\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}\end{subarray}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}=\sigma\right]]}]}~\leq~\eta,

where
\sigma\sim\mathbf{Z}_{N(U)}
is used to denote that
\sigma\in\Sigma^{N(U)}
is sampled according to the local distribution induced on
N(U)
by
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
.

That is, on average, we end up with an
\eta
-good pseudocodeword. We actually picked
\eta
to be much smaller than the bound on average covariance we will be needing, so that the probability (over conditionings) of obtaining a weaker low covariance becomes very close to 1. This is needed to be able to take a union bound with some other low-probability events we will see soon. A simple application of Markov’s inequality shows that the probability of obtaining an
\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}
-good pseudocodeword is at least
1-\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}
.

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{U,\sigma}{\mathbb{P}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}>\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}\right]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{U,\sigma}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}>\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{U,\sigma}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}>\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{U,\sigma}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}>\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}]}~\leq~\frac{4\eta}{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}~\leq~\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}\;.

(12)

Therefore, we started with a pseudocodeword that is close to
h
(
Eq.

10
), and then condition it to make it
\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}
-good. We must also argue that this conditioned pseudocodeword is still close to
h
, at least with some probability. This probability cannot be made too large, and this is why we needed to ensure that the low average correlation property holds with probability close to 1, so that both of these hold simultaneously with some positive probability. To do this, we use the law of total expectation and another application of Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{U,\sigma}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{U,\sigma}[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{U,\sigma}[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{U,\sigma}[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen]}~

\displaystyle=~\Delta_{R}({\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}},h)\;,

(13)

\displaystyle<~\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\varepsilon\;,

\displaystyle(\text{Using }\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{eqn:agreement_before_conditioning})

(14)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\mathchoice{\underset{U,\sigma}{\mathbb{P}}\left[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{U,\sigma}[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{U,\sigma}[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{U,\sigma}[\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}]}~

\displaystyle\geq~\frac{\varepsilon/2}{\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}~\geq~\frac{\varepsilon}{2\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}\;.

(15)

Using a union bound over
Eq.

12
and
Eq.

15
, we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{V,\sigma}{\mathbb{P}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}~\leq~\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}\text{ and }\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{V,\sigma}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}~\leq~\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}\text{ }\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{V,\sigma}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}~\leq~\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}\text{ }\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{V,\sigma}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]]}~\leq~\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}\text{ (16)

Suppose such a conditioning pair
(U,\sigma)
is chosen in
Algorithm

7.7
, and this happens with probability at least
\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}
.

We define
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}={\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot\given\mathbf{Z}_{U}=\sigma]}}
as in

, and let us call the corresponding covariance operator as

{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}
satisfies the following two properties:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\dupCov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\dupCov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\dupCov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\dupCov\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]]}~\leq~\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4}\;,

\displaystyle\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen~\leq~\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\;.

Using the SoS distance proof for AEL from

6.1
for
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}
, we can use the above upper bound on

to deduce an upper bound on

.

\displaystyle\Delta_{R}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen~\geq~\delta_{\mathrm{in}}-\frac{\lambda+\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}/4}{\Delta_{L,\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\quad\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}~\leq~\frac{\lambda+\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}/4}{\Delta_{L,\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\Delta_{L,\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen~\leq~\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}-\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4\left\lparen\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\right\rparen}~\leq~\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}-\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}\;.

We next wish to show that
y\in({\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{b_{\mathrm{out}}})^{L}
obtained by rounding
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
in step (iv) of

can be used to find the coset

.

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\in L}{\mathbb{E}}\left[~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in \displaystyle=\Delta_{L,\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}^{*}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen~\leq~\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}-\frac{\varepsilon\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}\,.

Using Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{y}{\mathbb{P}}\left[\Delta\left\lparen y,\overline{h}\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}\right]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{y}[\Delta\left\lparen y,\overline{h}\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{y}[\Delta\left\lparen y,\overline{h}\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}]}{{\mathbb{P}}_{y}[\Delta\left\lparen y,\overline{h}\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}]}\geq\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}\,.

Suppose a
y
is found in
Algorithm

7.7
such that
\Delta\left\lparen y,\overline{h}\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}
, which happens with probability at least
\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}
. Then the
X
-decoder of the
(\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
code must return the coset
\overline{h}+\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
. Let
\overline{z}
be a coset representative of
\overline{h}+\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
returned by the
X
-decoder of
(\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
. Using
\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}=\widetilde{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}}(\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp})+\mathbb{F}_{q}^{n}\otimes\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
from
Proposition

3.2
,

\displaystyle\overline{h}-\overline{z}~

\displaystyle\in~\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\;,

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}}(\overline{h}-\overline{z})~

\displaystyle\in~\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\;,

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}}(\overline{h})-\widetilde{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}}(\overline{z})~

\displaystyle\in~\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\;,

\displaystyle h-\widetilde{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}}(\overline{z})~

\displaystyle\in~\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}+{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{n}\otimes\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}=\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\;.

Therefore,
Algorithm

7.7
adds the coset
\widetilde{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}}(\overline{z})+\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}=h+\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
to
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
. In conclusion, if the following three events happen, the coset
h+\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
is added to the list
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
.

1.

u=u^{*}
is chosen, which happens with probability at least
\frac{\eta^{2}}{s^{3d}}
.

2.

The pair
(U,\sigma)
to condition on is chosen such that
Eq.

16
holds. Conditioned on previous event, this happens with probability at least
\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}
.

3.

A
y
is generated so that
\Delta\left\lparen y,\overline{h}\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}
. Conditioned on above two events, this happens with probability at least
\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}
.

Therefore, in any iteration, the coset
h+\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
is added to the list
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
with probability at least,

p~=~\frac{\eta^{2}}{s^{3d}}\cdot\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}\cdot\frac{\varepsilon}{4\delta_{\mathrm{in}}}~=~\frac{\left\lparen\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}\right\rparen^{2}\cdot\varepsilon^{6}}{4096\cdot s^{3d}\cdot\delta_{\mathrm{in}}^{4}}~=~\Omega_{s,d,\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}(\varepsilon^{6})\,.

Note that this immediately implies an upper bound of
1/p
on the list size, although we can get better list sizes (combinatorially) by appealing to the covering lemma and approximate Carathéodory theorem.

Finally, we show that with enough repetitions,
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
must contain the entire list
{\mathcal{L}}
with high probability. The probability that a coset in
{\mathcal{L}}
does not get added to
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
in
M
iterations is

(1-p)^{M}~\leq~e^{-p\cdot M}

With a union bound over the entire list, which is of size at most
1/p
, the probability that any coset in
{\mathcal{L}}
is not present in
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
is at most

\frac{1}{p}\cdot e^{-p\cdot M}~=~e^{-pM\ln p}~\leq~\gamma\;\text{ if }M=\frac{\ln(1/\gamma)}{p\ln p}.\qquad\hbox{\vrule width=6.0pt,height=6.0pt,depth=0.0pt}

7.3
Derandomization

In this section, we show how to derandomize the list decoding
Algorithm

7.7
. In this process, the size of the list
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
output by the algorithm may be significantly larger, and in particular may grow polynomially with
n
. In contrast, the list size for the probabilistic version was bounded by a constant independent of
n
. Once again, this derandomization is very similar to the one used by
[
JST23
]
, and we only include it for completeness.

Suppose we have the same setting as in the last section, and we borrow notation from there to avoid defining the same objects twice. We wish to show that
Algorithm

7.7
can be modified to deterministically output a list
{\mathcal{L}}^{\prime}
of polynomial size that contains the true list
{\mathcal{L}}
. There are three steps where
Algorithm

7.7
makes random choices, and we will address each of them separately:

1.

We try out all
\frac{s^{3d}}{\eta^{2}}
possible values of
u
instead of picking one randomly.

2.

We try all pairs
(U,\sigma)
to condition on. These are at most
n^{\frac{s^{3d}}{\eta^{2}}}\cdot s^{d\frac{s^{3d}}{\eta^{2}}}
many in number, which causes a multiplicative overhead of
(s^{d}n)^{\frac{s^{3d}}{\eta^{2}}}
in running time and list size.

3.

Finally, for independently rounded
y
, we use a standard technique called threshold rounding, also used in
[
JST23
]
, to try out all possible
y
that could be generated. This is described in the next
Lemma

7.8
.

7.8
.

Let
(\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
be the outer code defined over alphabet
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}
, which is unique decodable from distance
\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}\leq\delta/2
in time
{\mathcal{T}}(n)
. Given a pseudocodeword
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
with the property

\Delta_{L,\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen\leq\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}\;\;\text{ for some $h\in\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}}$,}

we can find the coset
h+\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
in time
{\mathcal{O}}(q^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}n)\cdot{\mathcal{T}}(n)
with a deterministic algorithm.

Proof.

We will use
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
to obtain
|L|=n
distributions over
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}
, say
\{{\mathcal{W}}_{\ell}~|~\ell\in L\}
. We will not use the vector space structure of
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}
here and only view it as a set of size
t:=q^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}
. We identify elements of
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}
with those of
[t]
under an arbitrary ordering, and henceforth, we will view each
{\mathcal{W}}_{\ell}
as a distribution over
[t]
. If
w_{\ell j}
denotes the weight of distribution
{\mathcal{W}}_{\ell}
on the symbol
j\in[t]
,

w_{\ell j}={\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\varphi_{X}^{-1}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})=j\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\varphi_{X}^{-1}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})=j\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\varphi_{X}^{-1}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})=j\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\varphi_{X}^{-1}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})=j\right\}}]}}

We round
\{{\mathcal{W}}_{\ell}~|~\ell\in L\}
to an
h^{\prime}\in({\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{b_{\mathrm{out}}})^{L}
vertex by vertex. Choose a random
\theta\in[0,1]
. For each
\ell\in L
,
h^{\prime}_{\ell}
is defined to be the smallest
j\in[t]
such that
\theta<w_{\ell 1}+w_{\ell 2}+\cdots+w_{\ell j}
.

As before, let
\overline{h}\in\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}}
be the codeword corresponding to
h\in\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}}
, that is,
\overline{h}_{\ell}={\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})
. Therefore, the probability of
h^{\prime}_{\ell}=j
is precisely
w_{\ell j}
, as before. These events across
\ell\in L
are no longer independent however. But linearity of expectation can be used to show that the key claim from the previous section, that the distance of
h^{\prime}
from
\overline{h}
is at most
\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}
, continues to hold under this modified rounding. If this holds,
h^{\prime}
can be used to find
h+\mathcal{E}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}
using the unique decoder of
(\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
.

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{\theta\in[0,1]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Delta(h^{\prime},\overline{h})\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta\in[0,1]}[\Delta(h^{\prime},\overline{h})]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta\in[0,1]}[\Delta(h^{\prime},\overline{h})]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta\in[0,1]}[\Delta(h^{\prime},\overline{h})]}~

\displaystyle=~\mathchoice{\underset{\theta\in[0,1]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta\in[0,1]}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta\in[0,1]}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta\in[0,1]}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{h^{\prime}_{\ell}\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}]}

\displaystyle=~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[1-w_{\ell\overline{h}_{\ell}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[1-w_{\ell\overline{h}_{\ell}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[1-w_{\ell\overline{h}_{\ell}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[1-w_{\ell\overline{h}_{\ell}}]}

\displaystyle=~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq\overline{h}_{\ell}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\sc \displaystyle=~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(\mathbf{Z}_{\ell})\neq{\varphi}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{{X}\mathstrut}}^{\raisebox{0.90417pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}(h_{\ell})\right\}}]}}]}

\displaystyle=~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[{{\mathbf{1}}\left\{\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\not\in h_{\ell}+\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}\right\}}]}}]}

\displaystyle=~\Delta_{L,\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}}^{\perp}}\left\lparen{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}\,,h\right\rparen~\leq~\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}\;.

The fact that a common threshold
\theta
shared by all
\ell\in L
is used to generate this rounding allows us to make a single random choice for
\theta
instead of different random choices for each
\ell\in L
. Moreover, the rounded
h^{\prime}
is exactly same if the threshold
\theta
is perturbed without crossing any of the
|L|\cdot t=q^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}\cdot n
values
\{w_{\ell 1}+w_{\ell 2}+\cdots w_{\ell j}\}_{\ell\in L,j\in[t]}
. Therefore, we only need to try out roundings for
q^{b_{\mathrm{out}}}\cdot n
many distinct thresholds, which proves the lemma.

7.4
Near-MDS Quantum LDPC Codes list decodable up to Johnson bound

In this section, we show how instantiating the AEL amplification with unique decodable asymptotically good QLDPC codes leads to QLDPC codes near the (quantum) Singleton bound that can be list decoded up to the Johnson bound.

7.9
Near-MDS Codes decodable upto Johnson bound.

For any
0<\rho<1
, and for any
\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}>\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}>0
, there is an infinite family of quantum LDPC codes
(\mathcal{F}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{F}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
with the following properties:

(i)

The rate of the code is at least
\rho
and distance is at least
\frac{1-\rho-\varepsilon_{1}}{2}
.

(ii)

The code is over an alphabet of size
2^{{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon_{1}^{-6}\log(1/\varepsilon_{1}))}
.

(iii)

The code of blocklength
n
can be list decoded from radius
{\mathcal{J}}(\frac{1-\rho-\varepsilon_{1}}{2})-\varepsilon_{2}
in time
n^{{\mathcal{O}}_{\varepsilon_{1}}(1/\varepsilon_{2}^{4})}
.

Proof.

We show how to instantiate
Theorem

7.6
to obtain such codes.
We will use the AEL distance amplification based on a family of
(n,d,\lambda)
-expanders, with
d
and
\lambda
to be chosen later.

The inner code
\mathcal{C}=(\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{C}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
is chosen to be a quantum Reed-Solomon code of rate
\rho_{0}:=\frac{\rho}{1-\varepsilon_{1}}
and distance
\frac{1-\rho_{0}}{2}
. Since the inner code has blocklength
d
, the alphabet size of
\mathcal{C}
must be at least
d
, and we choose
\mathcal{C}
to be defined over a finite field
{\mathbb{F}}_{2^{b_{\mathrm{in}}}}
, where
b_{\mathrm{in}}\leq 1+\log_{2}d
. Using
Lemma

2.13
, we will view
\mathcal{C}
as a vector space CSS code with alphabet
{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{b_{\mathrm{in}}}
.

For the outer code
\mathcal{D}=(\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{D}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
, we fold the binary QLDPC codes of
[
LZ23
]
into blocks of size
b_{\mathrm{out}}
, with
b_{\mathrm{out}}=b_{\mathrm{in}}\cdot\rho_{0}d
so that concatenation is well defined. The starting binary code from
[
LZ23
]
is chosen to have rate
1-\varepsilon_{1}
that can be unique decoded from radius
\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}=\Omega(\varepsilon_{1}^{2})
, and these properties are preserved after folding.

Let
\lambda=\varepsilon_{1}\delta_{\mathrm{dec}},\;
so that
\lambda\leq\Omega(\varepsilon_{1}^{3})
and
d={\mathcal{O}}(1/\varepsilon_{1}^{6})
. The rate of the AEL-amplified code
(\mathcal{F}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{F}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
is
(1-\varepsilon_{1})\rho_{0}=\rho
, and the distance is at least

\displaystyle\frac{1-\rho_{0}}{2}-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{1}}

\displaystyle~\geq~\frac{1}{2}\left\lparen 1-\frac{\rho}{1-\varepsilon_{1}}\right\rparen-\frac{\lambda}{\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}

\displaystyle~\geq~\frac{1}{2}\left\lparen 1-\rho-2\varepsilon_{1}\rho\right\rparen-\varepsilon_{1}

\displaystyle~\geq~\frac{1-\rho}{2}-2\varepsilon_{1}

The final code is defined over alphabet
{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{b_{\mathrm{in}}\cdot d}
, which is of size at most
2^{(1+\log d))d}=2^{{\mathcal{O}}\left\lparen\varepsilon_{1}^{-6}\log(1/\varepsilon_{1})\right\rparen}
. Since AEL amplification preserves the LDPC property of the outer code, our final code
(\mathcal{F}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{X\mathstrut}},\mathcal{F}_{\lx@scalerel@obj{Z\mathstrut}})
is also LDPC. For list decodability, we use
Theorem

7.6
to claim that the above code can be list decoded from
{\mathcal{J}}(\frac{1-\rho}{2}-2\varepsilon_{1})-\varepsilon_{2}
in time
n^{{\mathcal{O}}_{q,d,\delta_{\mathrm{dec}}}(1/\varepsilon_{2}^{4})}=n^{{\mathcal{O}}_{\varepsilon_{1}}(1/\varepsilon_{2}^{4})}
. The claimed parameters can be obtained by replacing
\varepsilon_{1}
by
\varepsilon_{1}/4
. We note that the decoder above can be made deterministic as described in
Section

7.3
.

Appendix A
Correlation Rounding

In this appendix, we include proofs of some claims that are standard when using correlation rounding for Sum-of-Squares hierarchy. This presentation is largely borrowed from
[
JST23
]
.

Recall that we are dealing with pseudocodewords as defined in
Section

5
, which can be seen as relaxations of certain distributions over
\Sigma^{E}
. Here,
\Sigma
is an alphabet of size
s
, and
E
is the edge set of a
d
-regular bipartite graph with
|L|=|R|=n
.

The following lemma says that the decrease in variance of one random variable upon conditioning on the other is proportional to their covariance. This is based on
[
BRS11
, Lemma 5.2]
, and is the basis of by now a standard technique used for rounding convex hierarchies.

A.1
.

Let
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
be a pseudoexpectation operator of SoS-degree
t
with associated pseudovariance
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\cdot]}}
and pseudocovariance
{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\cdot,\cdot\right]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}}
. Assume
S,T
are sets such that
|S|+|T|\leq t/2
, then,

{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{S}|\mathbf{Z}_{T}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{S}|\mathbf{Z}_{T}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{S}|\mathbf{Z}_{T}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{S}|\mathbf{Z}_{T}]}}\leq{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{S}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{S}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{S}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{S}]}}-\frac{1}{s^{|T|}}\sum_{\alpha\in\Sigma^{S},\beta\in\Sigma^{T}}\frac{({\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{S,\alpha},\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{S,\alpha},\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{S,\alpha},\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{S,\alpha},\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}]}})^{2}}{{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{T,\beta}]}}}

Note that since we are only talking about local sets
S
and
T
here, the argument follows by working only with the local distributions, which are true distributions.

The next lemma gives an averaged out version of the above.

A.2
.

Let
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
be a pseudocodeword of degree at least
4d
, with associated pseudovariance
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\cdot]}}
and pseudocovariance
{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\cdot,\cdot\right]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}}
. Then conditioning on a random
r\in R
causes a decrease in average pseudovariance in
L
that is proportional to the average pseudocovariance between
\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}
and
\mathbf{Z}_{r}
for
\ell,r
chosen uniformly from
L\times R
.

\mathchoice{\underset{r\in \displaystyle=\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}]}-\frac{1}{s^{3d}}\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left({\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}\right)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left({\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}\right)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left({\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}\right)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left({\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\ \displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}{{\widetilde{\mathrm{Var}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}]}}]}-\frac{1}{s^{3d}}\left(\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}\right]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}{{\tildecov}[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]}}]}\right)^{2}\qquad\hbox{\vrule width=6.0pt,height=6.0pt,depth=0.0pt}

That is, a pseudocodeword which is not
\eta
-good will see its average pseudovariance decrease by
\Omega(\eta^{2})
upon random conditioning. Because the (pseudo-)variance is bounded between 0 and 1, this can be used to obtain an
\eta
-good pseudocodeword after a small constant number of conditionings (although the number of conditionings will depend on
\eta
).

A.3
.

Given any pseudocodeword
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
of degree
\geq 2d\left(\frac{s^{3d}}{\eta^{2}}+1\right)
with associated pseudocovariance operator
{\mathchoice{{\tildecov}\left[\cdot,\cdot\right]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}{{\tildecov}[\cdot,\cdot]}}
and an
\eta>0
, there exists a number
u^{*}\leq s^{3d}/\eta^{2}
such that

\mathchoice{\underset{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u^{*}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u^{*}}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u^{*}}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u^{*}}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\math Proof.

Suppose not, so that

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\underset{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}|\mathbf{Z}_{r_{1}},\mathbf{Z}_{r_{2}},\cdots,\mathbf{Z}_{r_{u^{*}}}]]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{u}}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{ \displaystyle\forall u\in[s^{3d}/\eta^{2}]

Lemma

A.2
, for every

,

This is impossible, since all pseudovariances are bounded between 0 and 1.

Finally, the next lemma shows how to use the
\eta
-good property obtained above via random conditioning. Essentially, it allows us to write a pseudoexpectation of products as a product of pseudoexpectations. However, this can only be done when these pseudoexpectations are applied to products coming from a uniform measure on
L\times R
, since that is the kind of guarantee on pseudocovariance that
\eta
-good property gives us.

A.4
.

Let
\{X_{\ell}\}_{\ell\in L}
and
\{Y_{r}\}_{r\in R}
be two collections of
d
-local functions on
\Sigma^{E}
such that for every
\ell\in L
,
X_{\ell}(f)
only depends on
f_{\ell}
and for every
r\in R
,
Y_{r}(f)
only depends on
f_{r}
. Further, assume that
\lVert X_{\ell}\rVert_{\infty}\leq 1
and
\lVert Y_{r}\rVert_{\infty}\leq 1
for all
\ell\in L,r\in R
. Then, for an
\eta
-good pseudocodeword
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\cdot\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\cdot]}}
,

\displaystyle~=~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{\alpha,\beta}X_{\ell}(\alpha)Y_{r}(\beta)\cdot\left\lparen~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}-{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}}\cdot{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}~\right\rparen\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\sum_{\alpha,\beta}X_{\ell}(\alpha)Y_{r}(\beta)\cdot\left\lparen~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}-{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}}\cdot{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}~\right\rparen]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\sum_{\alpha,\beta}X_{\ell}(\alpha)Y_{r}(\beta)\cdot\le \displaystyle~\leq~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert X_{\ell}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert Y_{r}\rVert_{\infty}\cdot{\sum}_{\alpha,\beta}\left\lvert~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}-{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}}\cdot{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}~\right\rvert~\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\lVert X_{\ell}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert Y_{r}\rVert_{\infty}\cdot{\sum}_{\alpha,\beta}\left\lvert~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}-{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}}\cdot{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}~\right\rvert~]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\lVert X_{\ell}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert Y_{r}\rVert_{\infty}\cdot{\sum}_{\alpha,\beta}\left\lvert~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}-{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}}\cdot{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}~\right\rvert~]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\lVert X_{\ell}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert Y_{r}\rVert_{\infty}\cdot{\sum}_{\alpha,\beta}\left\lvert~{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}-{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(\ell),\alpha}]}}\cdot{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[\mathbf{Z}_{N(r),\beta}]}}~\right\rvert~]}

\displaystyle~\leq~\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\tildecov[\mathbf{Z}_{\ell},\mathbf{Z}_{r}]]}\leq\eta

Appendix B
Expander Mixing Lemma for SoS

In this section, we include proof of the well-known fact that SDPs such as the SoS hierarchy can reason with the expander mixing lemma (EML). We start by giving a version of EML for vector valued functions, which can be obtained by simply applying the usual EML coordinate-wise, along with an application of Cauchy-Schwartz inequality.

B.1
EML for vector-valued functions.

Let
\{v_{\ell}\}_{\ell\in L}
and
\{u_{r}\}_{r\in R}
be a collection of vectors in
{\mathbb{R}}^{N}
. Then,

\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}\right\rvert\leq\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert^{2}]}}\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}]}}

Proof.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\left\langle v_{\ell},u_{r}\right\rangle]}\right\rvert

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[\mathchoice{\underset{i\in[N]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i\in[N]}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}]}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\underset{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}\right\rvert\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}\right\rvert]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}\right\rvert]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}-\mathchoice{\underset{\ell,r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell,r}[v_{\ell}(i)u_{r}(i)]}\right\rvert]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\underset{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}}\cdot\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}}\cdot\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}}\cdot\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}}\cdot\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}}]}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[v_{\ell}(i)^{2}]}]}\cdot\mathchoice{\underset{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[u_{r}(i)^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[u_{r}(i)^{2}]}]}}

\displaystyle=\lambda\sqrt{\mathchoice{\underset{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell}[\lVert v_{\ell}\rVert]}^{2}\cdot\mathchoice{\underset{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{r}[\lVert u_{r}\rVert^{2}]}}

Next, we use the non-negativity of sum of squares of low-degree polynomials to show a certain matrix is positive semidefinite, allowing us to use the EML for vector-valued functions to prove the EML for pseudoexpectations. This is the only part in the entire proof that uses the non-negativity of sum-of-squares of low-degree polynomials. The rest of the argument can also be carried out with linear programming hierarchies for example. It is an interesting question whether list decoding can be carried out for AEL without the use of SDPs.

B.2
EML for pseudoexpectations.

Let
\{X_{\ell}\}_{\ell\in L}
and
\{Y_{r}\}_{r\in R}
be two collections of
d
-local functions on
\Sigma^{E}
such that for every
\ell\in L
,
X_{\ell}(f)
only depends on
f_{\ell}
and for every
r\in R
,
Y_{r}(f)
only depends on
f_{r}
.
Then for a
\lambda
-spectral expander, we have

\left\lvert\mathchoice{\underset{\ell\sim r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim r}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{\ell}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{r}(\mathbf{Z})]}}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\ell\sim Proof.

Let us use

and

as indices in

that can be used to index

and

. Consider the

{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})]}},&1\leq i\leq n,n+1\leq j\leq 2n\\
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}},&n+1\leq i\leq 2n,1\leq j\leq n\\
{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i-n}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j-n}(\mathbf{Z})]}}&n+1\leq i\leq 2n,n+1\leq j\leq 2n\end{cases}

We will prove that the matrix

is positive semidefinite for any collection of functions as in the lemma statement. To do this, we show that for any vector

, the quadratic form

, is non-negative.

\displaystyle v^{T}Mv

\displaystyle~=~\mathchoice{\underset{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[M_{ij}x_{i}x_{j}+M_{i,j+n}x_{i}y_{j}+M_{i+n,j}y_{i}x_{j}+M_{i+n,j+n}y_{i}y_{j}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i,j}[M_{ij}x_{i}x_{j}+M_{i,j+n}x_{i}y_{j}+M_{i+n,j}y_{i}x_{j}+M_{i+n,j+n}y_{i}y_{j}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i,j}[M_{ij}x_{i}x_{j}+M_{i,j+n}x_{i}y_{j}+M_{i+n,j}y_{i}x_{j}+M_{i+n,j+n}y_{i}y_{j}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i,j}[M_{ij}x_{i}x_{j}+M_{i,j+n}x_{i}y_{j}+M_{i+n,j}y_{i}x_{j}+M_{i+n,j+n}y_{i}y_{j}]}

\displaystyle~=~\mathchoice{\underset{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}}x_{i}x_{j}+{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}}x_{i}y_{j}+{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}}y_{i}x_{j}+{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}}y_{i}y_{j}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{i,j}[{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}}x_{i}x_{j}+{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[X_{i}(\mathbf{Z})Y_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}}x_{i}y_{j}+{\mathchoice{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}\left[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})\right]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}}[Y_{i}(\mathbf{Z})X_{j}(\mathbf{Z})]}{{\wid
